1a1(rgyud): @#/na mo bud+d+ha ya/na mo d+harmA ya/na maHsaM g+hA ya//
1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya nI laM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni ru dra tri bA ni ta n+t+ra nA ma/
1b2(rgyud): bod skad du/'phags pa lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can drag po gsum 'dul zhes bya 
1b3(rgyud): ba'i rgyud/bcom ldan 'das 'jam pa'i rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag 
1b4(rgyud): gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das bdud rnam par 'joms pa 
2a1(rgyud): @##//bdud kyi sde dang lhan cig tu 'og min gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi pho brang na thabs gcig tu 
2a2(rgyud): bzhugs so//gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can sangs rgyas gang gA'i klung gi bye ma 
2a3(rgyud): snyed kyi rjes su gnang ba 'di lag na rdo rje zung cig ces bka' stsal to//de nas phyag na rdo rjes gsol pa/gnod 
2a4(rgyud): sbyin bye ba stong phrag bye ba dpag tu med pa'i tshogs kyis yongs su bskor ba/mche ba gtsigs pa dang 'tsham pa/
2a5(rgyud): mig me bdag gi phung po 'dra ba/dbugs me lce ltar 'bar ba/'jigs pa'i nga ro can/padam dang chu 
2b1(rgyud): lhas lag pa dang dpung pa rgyan pa//nor rgyas dang dung skyong gis dbu la rgyan pa//sbrul rin po che 'bar ba'i cod pan thogs pa/klu dga' 
2b2(rgyud): bo dang nye dga' bo 'bar bas sman cha byas pa/rkang pas sbal gdug pa 'bar ba mnan cing za ba//klu'i rgyal po 'dab bzang rka 
2b3(rgyud): rags su bcings pa//sbrul nag po se ral khar thog pa bzhin rgyas la//smin ma kha gnyer du bsdus pa/ya sos ma mchu mnan 
2b4(rgyud): pa/lce glog ltar 'gyu ba/gshin rje'i tshe byad can/gsus po che sbrul za ba/rgya mtsho'i zabs su dpag tshad brgyad khri 
2b5(rgyud): yod pas rkang pa'i pus mor phyin pa dbang po dang nyi ma'i steng na bzhugs pa na/lag pa brdabs pa'i sgras 'jigs par byed pa/a 
2b6(rgyud): la la zhes bsgrags pas skrag par byed pa/klu thams cad brlag par byed pa/gad rgyangs chen po rgod pas kun tu 'khrug 
3a1(rgyud): @##//par byed pa/gshin rje'i gzugs can khams gsum 'jigs par byed pa/klu rnams 'dul bar 
3a2(rgyud): byed pa de la phyag 'tshal lo zhes byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rjes bstod na gsung du gsol zhes smras so//de nas nam mkha'i de 
3a3(rgyud): nyid la gzhal yas khang dang /'khor lo chen po'i ting nge 'dzin dang ldan pa'i drag po chen pos 'khor bsdus nas/phyag na rdo rje drag 
3a4(rgyud): po che||rdo rje khro bo drag chen 'joms//rdo rje gzi brjid sngags kyi gtso//rdo rje mi thub 'jigs byed che//rdo rje phyin las kun don 
3a5(rgyud): mdzad//thabs 'dul 'khor lo che sku la//'byung ba lnga yi bdag nyid che//mdzes ldan 'od zer can dang ni//mdangs can 
3a6(rgyud): sgra dbyangs rlung 'byin te/bskyed byed las kun sgrub pa las//'gugs shing sdud dang sdom zhing mdzad//mtha' rtul las 
3b1(rgyud): rnams sgrub pa la//thig le'i rang bzhin mi shigs la//kha sbyor nyi zla'i 'phro 'du las//bgegs rigs stong phrag 'joms mdzad pa//'khor 
3b2(rgyud): lo'i bdag po khyod la bstod//sa bon rab tu 'bar ba yis//thabs dang shes rab sreg gso mdzad//bde chen rab tu gsol mdzad nas//sdug bsngal 
3b3(rgyud): nyon mongs 'joms la 'dud//oM a kA ro mu khaM sar+b+ba d+har+m+ma NI A d+ya nu ta pan+na twA ta oM AHhUM//zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis dkyil 'khor gyi lha la 
3b4(rgyud): bstod nas 'jud do//byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje la legs so zhes bya ba byin nas 'khor rnams spros te/de nas phyag na rdo rjes bcom ldan 
3b5(rgyud): 'das legs so legs so//de'i phyir 'di gsang sngags las thams cad sgrub pa//sdig pa thams cad 'joms pa//drag po gsum 'dul ba 'di gsung 
3b6(rgyud): du gsol zhes brjod nas bskor ba byas te spyan sngar 'khor rnams dang lhan cig tu 'jug go/de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang ga'i klung gi bye ma 
3b7(rgyud): snyed kyi gsungs shing byin gyis rlabs pa'i rig sngags 'di gdug pa can rnams 'dul ba'i don du brjod par bya'o//tad+ya thA/b+ho d+ho pa nA ga a d+haM pa ta 
4a1(rgyud): @##//ye|i daM nI lAm+pa ra d+ha ra sha rI raM/badzra pA Ni hr-i da yaM yasa mA ti kra ma tha/mA ku ba uta sA da na b+ha bi ny+ya ti/tad+ya thA/bud+d+ha bud+d+ha bi 
4a2(rgyud): bud+d+ha bi bud+d+ha/ma ti kra ma tha/ha ra ha ra nA gA nAM/pra ha ra pa ha ra nA gA nAM/ts+tshin+da ts+tshin+da nA ga hr-i da yA ni/b+hai da b+hi de nA ga marma Ni/bi shIn+ti nA ga sha rI rA 
4a3(rgyud): Ni|bi s+pho Ta ya nA ga na ya nA ni/tad+ya thA/hUM phaT/nA ga bi dA+e ra NA ya phaT/nA ga ta sA da nA ya phaT/nI lA ba sa nA ya phaT/a pri ta ha ta ba le pa ra kra 
4a4(rgyud): mA ya phaT/kri tAM tA ya phaT/kr-i tAn ta ru pA ya phaT/ro Sha NA ya phaT/trA sa nA ya phaT/kuSh+mA Na ho sA da na ya phaT/pre ta pi sha tsi ba nA sha ka ra ya 
4a5(rgyud): phaT/yak+Sha a pasma ra bi nA sha ka rA ya phaT/ha sa ha sa/d+ha ra d+ha ra/mA ra ya mA ra ya nA/nA sha ya nA sha ya/sar+b+ba bag+h+na bi na sha ka rA ya phaT/
4a6(rgyud): sar+b+ba sha tAM saMg+ha bi nA sha ka rA ya phaDa/nI lAm+ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni rA dzA pa ya ti swAhA//de'i cho ga ni gnod sbyin gyi gdon gyis zin pa la'i chu la sogs btab te 
4a7(rgyud): gtor na btang bar 'gyur ro//klu bzlog par byed na smag shad kyi lcu ga ma 'am/bal po se'u yi lcug mas bya'o//yi dgas dang brjed byed rnams la ni 
4b1(rgyud): skud pas bya'o//rims nad med par bya ba dang /gzhogs phyed na ba dang /yi ga 'chus pa la ni skud pa sngon pos bya'o//nor 'dod pa la ni zhog gi bsnyungs par 'dug 
4b2(rgyud): ste spos kyis gdugs na rmi lam du dge ba dang mi dge ba'i don thams cad ston no//mchod rten sku gdung dang ldan pa'i drung du me tog sngon po la sogs pas mchod pa sngags stong bzlas 
4b3(rgyud): nas gsol ba btab na gser gyi dong tshe bdun stong sbyin par byed do//rgyas btab pa 'gol bar 'dod na thal bas bya'o//sgo lcags dbye bar 'dod na nyungs kar gyis bya'o//
4b4(rgyud): 'jigs pa thams cad las srung ba la ni rtsi mar snying pa dang thal ba dang nyungs kar gyis bya'o/nad kyis 'jigs pa thams cad la ni skud pa sngon po la srung ba'i mdud pa byas te 
4b5(rgyud): mgul du btags na srung ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ro//byis pa gdon gyi nyen pa thams cad la ni tshon sna lnga ba'i skud pa mgul la btags na srung ba'i mchag tu 'gyur ro//
4b6(rgyud): chom rkun bzlog par 'dod nas sa bya'o//dug la ni rtswa ku shas byab par bya'o//bdag srung ba la ni yid kyis sam chus bya'o//gzhan srung ba la ni skud pa sngon pos bya'o//
4b7(rgyud): las de dag thams cad la sngags brjod pa ni gcig gam/gsum mam/lan bcu bzhi 'am/nyi shu rtsa gcig gam/brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas na 'grub par 'gyur ro/
5a1(rgyud): @#//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje dang /bdud dpung dang bcas pa rjes su yi rang ste bstan pa la 'khod do//'phags 
5a2(rgyud): pa lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can drag po gsum 'dul ba zhes bya ba'i rgyud rdzogs so////bal po'i paN+Di ta de ba pu N+yA ma ti dang /lo ts+tsha ba dge slong chos kyi bzang pos bsgyur ba'o////
5a3(rgyud): rgya gar skad du/ru dra ti tan+t+ra gu h+ya bi wa ra ti bA ne ya nA ma/bod skad du/gsang ba'i rgyud rnams kyi rnam par 'byed pa drag po gsum 'dul zhes bya ba/'jam pa'i rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo/
5a4(rgyud): de nas phyag na rdo rje yis//gsang ba'i don 'di yang dag zhus//sgrub dang gnas dang mtshan nyid dang //rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis bka' stsal pa//shes rab snying rje gsang dbang 
5a5(rgyud): ldan||lcags 'dra'i ri dang bya skyibs dang //nags tshal mthug dang mchod rten nang //zhal gsum phyag drug 'jigs pa che//mche ba gcig pa nag po ste//dkar po g.yas 
5a6(rgyud): te khros pa'i mdog/hUM sgrogs ma lce 'bar ba'i dbugs//dmar ser g.yon te bzhad pa'i zhal//spyan dmar me dpung dang 'dra ba//zla ba rgyas pa lta bu'i zhal//nor rgyas dung skyong 
5a7(rgyud): ser pos dbu//dga' bo nye dga' snyan cha dmar//dpung pa padam chu lha dkar//se ral 'dab bzang rigs ldan gnag/tsham ngam rdo rje bsdigs mdzub dang //thal mos bdud 'joms 
5b1(rgyud): sbrul nag 'dzin//zhal gsum pa ste 'jigs pa che//sku gsum dag gis rab tu mtshan//pho nya gsum gyis gdug pa 'dul//klu yi gnyen po'i gnod sbyin bdag/bdud 'dul tshangs 
5b2(rgyud): pa'i pho nya ni//zla ba nyi ma dag gi steng //glang chen kar ka gser gyi gdan//brgya byin ser po rdo rje 'dzin//tshangs pa ral gri padam 'dzin//klu 'dul sbrul nag 'dzin pa'o//bdud 'dul 
5b3(rgyud): dbang po nyi ma'i steng //gnod sbyin zla dang stobs kyi steng //klu 'dul ri steng rgya mtsho'i nang //sbrul nag 'khyil pa stobs ldan te//mkha' dang ri bos mnan pa'i steng //gser mdog stobs 
5b4(rgyud): ldan gnyis kyi gdan//brgyad khrir zhabs bzhag ri rab mnan//me dpung sku la 'khrugs pa'i 'od//khams gsum khyab mdzad khro bo 'phro//sangs rgyas 'dus pa'i sku la phyag 'tshal 
5b5(rgyud): lo||de nas gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor ni//rdo rje me dpung 'od dang ldan//de nyid mkha' dang 'byung ba las//dur khrod brgyad dang ldan pa'i gnas//bdag po'i sa bon las byung ba//zla nyi 
5b6(rgyud): thod pa rdo rje las//drag po'i rtags ldan gtso bo che//mdzes ldan sku la yang dag 'khril//drag chen 'dul mdzad dri za'i gtso//mdog dkar rtse gsum 'bar ba 'dzin//'od 
5b7(rgyud): 'phro dkar mo sku la 'khril//'joms mdzad pu gri 'bar ba 'dzin//mgon po gzi brjid gshin rje'i bdag/mdog ser dbyug thos gshin rje 'dul//mdangs ldan ser mo 
6a1(rgyud): @#//sku la 'khril//'ja' 'dra ba yi zangs dri bsnams//'brug sgra bsgrogs pa'i klu yi gtso//mdog dmar klu 'dul sbrul gyi zhags//sgra yi lha mo sku la 
6a2(rgyud): 'khril||mdog dmar gcod pa'i ste'u bsnams//phrin las khro bo gnod sbyin bdag/ljang nag be tsa na gnod sbyin 'dul//rlung 'byin ljang ku sku la 'khril//'bar ba'i 
6a3(rgyud): 'khor lo phyag na bsnams//'gugs pa lcags kyu lcags kyu ste//sdud pa zhogs pa zhags pa'i bdag/sdom pa lcags sgrog lcags sgrog ste//zhi mdzad dril bu dril bu zhi//
6a4(rgyud): kha sbyor bad dang longs spyod dang //chos dang sprul pa'i 'khor lo la//sa bon rab tu 'bar ba yis//thabs dang shes rab sreg gso mdzad//nyi ma zla ba'i 'phro 'du las//thig 
6a5(rgyud): le'i rang bzhin mi gshigs la//gsang ba'i rgyud kyi rnam par 'byed pa drag po gsum 'dul zhes bya ba las le'u dang po'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//gsang ba'i 
6a6(rgyud): sngags ni khyod kyis zung //las rnams thams cad 'grub pa dang //sdig pa dag ni zhi ba'i phyir//klu dang gnod sbyin bsdigs pa dang //bdud rnams ma lus zhi byed pa'i//dus gsum 
6a7(rgyud): sangs rgyas byin rlabs pa'i//rdo rje can gyi sngags bshad bya//tad+ya thA/b+ho b+ho pan nA ga a d+hi pa ta ye/i daM nI lAm+ba ra d+ha ra sha rI raM/badzra pA Ni hr-i da yaM/yaHsa ma ti kra ma tha/mA ku la 
6b1(rgyud): u ta sA da naM/b+ha bi Sh+yan+ta/tad+ya thA/bud+d+ha bud+d+ha/bi bud+d+ha bi bud+d+ha/mA ti kA ma tha/ha ra ha ra nA gA nAM/pra ha ra pra ha ra nA gA nAM/ts+tshin+da ts+tshin+da nA ga hr-i da yA ni/b+hin+da b+hin+da nA ga marmA Ni bi shI r+yaM tu nA ga sha rI rA 
6b2(rgyud): Ni|be s+phoT ya/nA ga na ya nA ni/tad+ya thA hUM phaT/nA ga bi dA rA NA ya hUM phaT/nA go ta sA da nA ya phaT/nI la ba sa nA ya phaT/a pra ta ha ta ba la pa rA kra mA ya phaT/kri tAM tA ya phaT/kr-i tAM tA rU pA 
6b3(rgyud): ya phaT/ro Sha NA ya phaT/ta sa nA ya phaT/ku smAM Dota sA da nA ya phaT/pra ta pi shA tse baM nA sha ka rA ya phaT/yak+Sha a pasmA ra bi na sha ka ra ya phaT/ha sa ha sa/d+har d+ha ra/mA ra ya mA ra ya/nA sha 
6b4(rgyud): ya nA sha ya/sar+b+ba big+hAM binA sha ka rA ya phaT/sar+b+ba sha tUM saM g+ha bi nA sha ka rA ya phaT/nai lAm+ba ra d+ha ra badzra bA Ni rA dz+nyA ba ya ti swAhA//zhes pa phreng ba'i gsang ba'i sngags so//sangs rgyas gang 
6b5(rgyud): ga'i klung gis gsungs pa'i rig sngags 'di ni/sdig pa thaMd 'dag pa dang /ma rungs pa thams cad 'joms pa/las thaMd 'grub pa yin te/de yang rig pa 'dzin pas sgrub 
6b6(rgyud): ste dam tshig dang ldan pas so//de yang bla ma la gus shing //yi dam dkon mchog la dad cing //byang chub sems dang sdom pa dang //lha dang sngags la gus pa dang //rgyun tu rnal 
6b7(rgyud): 'byor dang ldan te//rgyud don don la brtson ldan na//las rnams thams cad 'grub pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rjes bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' bzhin mdzad pa bac+doMs 
7a1(rgyud): @#//la zhus pa/dam tshig dag las 'das na las rnams mi 'grub/dag pa med don med dag ste las rnams mi 'grub/'bras bu gang du skye zhes zhus/rdo rje'i 
7a2(rgyud): dmyal ba'i nang du mi bzad pa'i 'bras bur 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das de'i tshe'i tshid ji tsam du sdug bsngal myong bar 'gyur zhes zhus pas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
7a3(rgyud): chen po phyag na rdo rje khyod legs so//skrag par ma byed cig/'dis ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs bshig ste/thugs dang 'gal bas rdo rje gdan zhig tsam na thub 
7a4(rgyud): par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan mdas rdo rje'i gdan nam 'jig par 'gyur zhes zhus pa dang /nam mkha'i khams kyi sems can thams cad sangs rgyas pa'i 'bras bu thob ste rdo rje gdan 'jig 
7a5(rgyud): ste thar bar 'gyur ro//bar ma do dang skye gnas 'jig pa dag yod//de'i gsang ba'i sngags ni zla ba'i steng du yi ge a bkod/chos nyid dag pa'i sngags brjod//na maHsa ma 
7a6(rgyud): ta badzra NAM/sar+b+ba d+harmA mi ta badzra sa twa hUM zhes phyogs bcur kha bltas te rdo rje thal mo sbyar la ye shes kyi 'od kyis gsol ba btab la rdo rje sems dpa'i skur bya'o//oM badzra satwa hUM 
7a7(rgyud): zhes pa 'bum brjod//rtsa ba'i dam tshig nyams pa zhi bar 'gyur ro//rdo rje rdo rje 'jigs byed che//me long me long gsal ba'i sku//zhal gsum phyag drug 'jigs pa che//
7b1(rgyud): mtshon cha 'jig la phyag 'tshal bstod//de bzhin gshegs pa de bzhin gshegs//chos dbyings chos dbyings rnam par dag/zhal gsum phyag drug mtshon cha 'dzin//de bzhin 
7b2(rgyud): gshegs la phyag 'tshal bstod//so so kun rtog kun rtog bral//padam dri med padama'i bdag/zhal gsum phyag drug 'tshon cha 'dzin//rgya mtsho skyes sku phyag 'tshal bstod//rin 
7b3(rgyud): chen bai DUr gi za brjid 'bar//mnyam pa mnyam nyid pa shes sku//zhal gsum phyag drug mtshan cha 'dzin//rin chen rgya mtsho phyag 'tshal bstod//las kyi sku ste phrin las mdzad//
7b4(rgyud): bya ba bya ba sgrub pa'i bdag/zhal gsum phyag drug mtshon che 'dzin//phrin las rgya mtsho phyag 'tshal bstod //oM a nan+ta pa ra swAhA/oM su m+b+ha na sum+b+ha hUM/A kar+Sha ya baM d+ha ya swAhA//
7b5(rgyud): ha ya gri ba ban+d+ha sho ka swAhA/oM a pa ra a pa rI dza ta swAhA/ro Sha Na ye b+h+yaHphaT/de nas lha'i gsang sngags brjod par bya'o//oM badzra kro d+ha tre lokya baM dza ya hUM phaT/mA ra ya tsit+ta rbad 
7b6(rgyud): rbad|oM badzra kro d+ha ma hA kA la ya hUM phaT/tsit+ta la kro d+hi duny+dza/oM badzra kro d+ha nA ga hUM phaT/mA ra yarak+Sha rdubs rdubs oM badzra kro d+ha a mr-i te hUM phaT/mA ra ya sod sod rbad/de nas lha ma 
7b7(rgyud): srin sde brgyad kyis rig pa phul ba/mahA de ba mA ra ya sod sod/mahA dak+Sha a su ra mA ra ya ha na ha na/nA ga ra dza mA ra ya dzaHdzaHmahA ma ni mA ra ya mA ra ya rdubs rdubs/mahA b+h+ruM 
8a1(rgyud): @#//mA ra ya tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/mahA yak+Sha mA ra ya b+hin+da b+hin+da/kharga mA ra ya khos khos/mahA ta bah+ma de ye phaT/mA ra Na sod sod/mA ra Na bying bying /mA ra Na mugs mugs/mA 
8a2(rgyud): ra Na ha la ha la/mA ra Na d+hu na d+hu na/mA ra Na myos myos/mA ra Na bying bying /thum ri li li/thum ri li li/dzaHdzaHbum pa legs par ldan pa yis//sman gsum rtsi sman sna tshogs 
8a3(rgyud): dang|kha di ri yi lo 'dab bcas//lcags kyi rdo rje'i khong song du//gsal ba'i sngags ni brjod par bya//gnam lcags mchu ni stong pa ru//sprin bral ser ba bcad pa'i phyir 
8a4(rgyud): gsang pa'i sngags 'di brjod par bya'o//tad+ya thA/oM Tak+ki tsa ti gu h+ya la mi Sha sha gu gu/shi taM bi ti/hu ya hu ya pU ra ta swAhA/oM badzra pA Ni badzrA Da ki nA ga te he/hu ya hu ya/hu lu 
8a5(rgyud): hu lu|pu ru te pu ru te swAhA/oM many+dzu shrI ya mAn+te ka/e ka a pa ra hUM phaT/oM ma NiM pad+me be sha pra be sha mA ma ru lu be sha kha thun b+h+yo/oM bah+ma swa sti/puSh+TiM su ra kau shi ka/rAM dra i 
8a6(rgyud): shra ra|biSh+Nu/i sha ni/ku mA ra shaM ka ra/sma ra Sha trUM swAhA/oM karko Ta/ku li sha/pad+ma/mahA pad+ma/a nan+ta/tak+Sha ka/bA su ki/sha kha pA la/nA ga rA dza swAhA/oM A di t+ya/
8a7(rgyud): so ma aM ga/bu d+haHbaM ha swa ti/shu kaHsha nai sh+tsa ra/ke tu swAhA/oM nak+Sha tra/karti ka/ro hi NaM/mr-i ga shi raHa rdaHpu narba suH/pu Sh+yaHa sh+le ShaH/ma g+ha/pUrba phal gu 
8b1(rgyud): cI|uta tar phal gu NI/tastA tsita swa ti/bi shA khA/a nu rA d+hA/d+yaSh+Tha/mU laM pUrbaShA D+hA/ut+tA rA Sha D+hA/shra ba NaHa b+hi dzi ta/sha ta b+hi ShA d+ha niShaTaHA/pUrba dra pA dA/ut+ta ra b+ha dra pA da/rai 
8b2(rgyud): ba ti a shwi nI b+ha ra NI swAhA/oM in+d+ra ya/oM ya ma ye ra ka sa/ba ru Na/ku be ra/ag+ni rAk+Sha/bA ya be/I sha na sUr+ya/tsan+d+ra lo ka pA lA ya swAhA/bi shra ba Na/d+h+y-i ta raSh+Ta/bi ru 
8b3(rgyud): D+ha ka|bi ru pAk+Sha swAhA/oM de ba nA ga rA dza su ra gaM d+harba sh+tsa/ga ru Da/kin+na ra/ma ho ra ga/kuM b+hAN+Da swAhA/ha ra ha ra/nA ga phuH/ts+tshin+d+ra ts+tshin+da/nA ga hr-i da yA ni phuH/b+hin+da b+hin+da/nA ga 
8b4(rgyud): marma Ni phuH/bi shIr+ya nA ga sha rI rA Ni phuH/bi s+pho Ta ya nA ga na man ni phuH/nA ga bi dA ra NA ya phuH/nA ga ta sI da nA ya phuHswAhA/yak+Sha ba dA ra NA ya hUM phaT/yak+Sha kro d+ha sA da nA ya hUM phaT/
8b5(rgyud): yak+Sha nI la ba sa nA ya   phaT swAhA/yak+Sha a pa ti ha ta ba lA ya phaT/yak+Sha pa rA kra mA ya phaT/yak+Sha kr-i tAM tA ya phaT/yak+Sha kr-i tA n+te rU pA ya phaT/yak+Sha ro Sha NA ya phaT/de skad ces 
8b6(rgyud): bka' stsal nas thams cad dbang du 'dus par 'gyur ro//gsang ba'i rgyud kyi rnam par 'byed pa drag po gsum 'dul zhes bya ba las gsang ba'i sngags kyi le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
9a1(rgyud): @#//sgrub po dag ni bsnyun ldan pas//rmi bltas rim pa gsal ba yis//nad pa dag la nyung sngags so//bcom ldan mgon po zhal gsum dmar//phyag drug 
9a2(rgyud): rdo rje bsdigs pa g.yon//drag po'i thal mos 'jigs par byed//tha ma sbrul gdug ri rab mnan//'khor du gnod sbyin chen po ste//sha za rab 'jigs ral ba can//
9a3(rgyud): gdong ser ser gdong gnod sbyin mo//bong bu'i rna ba rna ba 'phyang /ral pa gyen 'greng ral gri so//gnod sbyin 'khor de lnga brgya bcas//yak+Sha bi dA ra NA ya hUM phaT/yak+Sha kro d+ha sa d+ha nA ya 
9a4(rgyud): hUM phaT/yak+Sha nI laM ba sa nA ya hUM phaT/yak+Sha pra ti ha ta ba lA ya hUM phaT/yak+Sha pa rA kra mA ya phaT/yak+Sha kr-i tAn ta rU pA ya phaT/yak+Sha ra kha NA ya phaT/ces brjod de tshul sngags 
9a5(rgyud): shing gtor na gnod sbyin gyi gdon las thar//mchod rten sku gdung ldan pa'i drung //tshon sna lnga dang ldan pa yis//mchod pa lnga dang ldan pas mchod/sgrub po dag ni bsnyun 
9a6(rgyud): ldan pas//rmi ltas rim pa gsal ba yi//nad pa dag la tshon snas bcing //bcom ldan mgon po zhal gsum ldan//phyag drug rdo rje bsdigs pa ga yin//drag po thal 
9a7(rgyud): mos 'jigs par byed//tha ma sbrul gdug ri rab mnan//mkhas la rdzu 'phrul dang ldan bdud//dga' rab dbang phyug thad phreng can//bun sna sen dgu sha za bdud//
9b1(rgyud): btsun mo me tog mdzes pa dang //'od zer 'phrog dang mdangs 'phrog ma//gdon rigs stong phrag brgyad cu'i 'khor//ma lus bcas pa'i sngags 'di ni//gnad la bor ba'i srog 
9b2(rgyud): sngags so//pre ta pi shA tsi be dA ra d+ha ya hU phaT/pre ta pi shA tsa kro d+ha sA dA nA ya hU phaT/pre ta bi shA tsa nI la ba sa nA ya hUM phaT/pre ta bi sha tsa a pra ti ha ta ba mA ya phaT/pre ta pi shA ci 
9b3(rgyud): pa rA kra mA ya phaT/pre ta bi shA ci kr-i tAn ta rU pA ya phaT/pre ta bi sha ci ro Sha NA ya phaT/nyi ma zla ba dag gi bar du rlung la bskyon te//tshin sna lngas bcings te bskrad par bya'o//mchod 
9b4(rgyud): rten sku gdung ldan pa'i drung //maN+Dal me tog bcas pa'i steng //bsrung bas khyi yi shal sgrub//rmi ltas rim pa ldan pas brtag/brgyad khri zhabs nub 'phags pa'i me//gad 
9b5(rgyud): rgyangs bzhad mdzad zhal gsum pa//nag pa zhabs gdub gdug pa 'dzin//stobs chen rab tu gnon pa po//klu chen ma rungs gdug pa can//nor rgyas dang ni dung skyong dang /
9b6(rgyud): dga' bo dang ni 'jog po dang //padam padam chen po'o//stobs kyi rgyu dang rigs ldan rnams//rgya mtsho dag la gnas pa yi//klu dang gnyan dangs bdag sum//sprul pa las la grub 
9b7(rgyud): ba yi//glen pa nga rgyal rdzu 'phrul can//sprul pa 'bum sde 'khor bcas pa'i//ma lus pa yi sngags 'di ni//gnad la bor da'i rtsa sngags so//ha ra ha ra nA ga phu/
10a1(rgyud): @#//pra ha ra pra ha ra nA ga phu/ts+tshin+da ts+tshin+da nA ga phu/b+hin+da b+hin+da nA ga phu/bi shIr+ya nA nA ga sha rI rA Ni phu/bi s+pho Ta ya nA ga na ya nA ni phu/nA ga pi dA ra NA ya phu/nA ga kro 
10a2(rgyud): d+ha sA da nA ya phu/bal po se'u dag dang ni//spag shod dag gi lcug mas bya//de ni klu bzlog pa'i cho ga'o//nA ga du na dus/nA ga she na dus/hi li hi li nA ga dus dus/klu chen 
10a3(rgyud): nA ga'i srog snying ste//gnad la bong ba'i srog snying ngo//lco 'phye nag gi kha nas khar//bcom ldan sku ni gsal bas bzlas//de nas rdo rje 'dzin la bka' stsal pa/las rnams 
10a4(rgyud): bzhi yis las rnams 'grub//sa gzhi dag ni sor brtags zhi ba dkar zlum padam can//dbang dmar zla gam lcags kyu ste//drag po mthing nag rtse gsum pa'o//zhi ba rgyas pa 
10a5(rgyud): sor bzhi dang //khru dang khru ngo mnyam pa'o//dbang dang drag po sor gsum ste//khru ngo khru gang khru gsum khru//'o ma can dang dri zhim zhing //'bras bu dang bcas zhi rgyas zhing //tshed 
10a6(rgyud): ma dur khrod shing dag ni//kha dog dmar nag dbang drag la//ku sha dag kyang dgram byas te//las kyi dum pas gsang bar bya'o//zhi ba zho dang mar dang ni//'o ma dri zhim 
10a7(rgyud): me tog dkar//'bras dang nas dang gro dang ni//zho yi cha dang bsres pa dbul//rgyas pa zho sbags dkar gsum ste//mngar gsum 'bras bu gsum dag ste//yam shing 'o ma lco 
10b1(rgyud): 'dab bcas//til ni dkar po shis pa'o//khra dang 'ug pa bya rog brun//ske tshe nim pa nyungs kar dang //khrag gis sbags pa'i rdzas kun te//yam shing tsher ma mar dag dang //sha dang 
10b2(rgyud): tshil dang gzugs bcas pa//til ni nag po shis pa'o//shar du bltas na zhi ba ste//byang du bltas na rgyas pa'o//nub tu bltas na dbang du sdud//lho ru bltas te bsad par bya'o//
10b3(rgyud): sbyin sreg byas pas las kun grub//g.yas brkyang gar gyis gdug pa ste//g.yon brkyang bas ni gsad pa'o//bum pa kha dog sngon po ste//gnam lcags sor ni bcu dug tshad//sman 
10b4(rgyud): lnga 'bru lnga rin po che//'bras gsum 'bru gsum bcas pa ste//dri zhim lo 'dab bcas pa'i shing //ba yi rnam lnga khru ngo tshad//gru bzhi khru gang zlum po ste//seng ldang bzhi las byas 
10b5(rgyud): pa yi//phur ba tshan sna dang bcas pa'i//dkar gsum mngar gsum bcas pa yi//gdengs ka can la dbul bar bya'o//grub pa khrus bya bsrungs bar bya'o//'bras chan bu ram 
10b6(rgyud): dwags dang ni//'bru yi chan dang dri zhim dang //dkar gsum mngar gsung chu dag dang //mdun du byas te phyogs rnams su//sha dang khrag dang rus pa dang //nas dang 'bras dang gra la 
10b7(rgyud): sogs//la phug sgeg pa 'bru la sogs//chang dang bcas pa mdun du dbul//oM bah+ma/oM buSh+Ta/oM ru dA ya/oM karti ka/oM gaN pa ti/oM in+d+rA ya/oM ma hA 
11a1(rgyud): @#//kA la|oM mahA brah+ma ye/ba li ta ta le/ba li ta ta le swAhA//stag dang khab leb ti che dang /maN+Dal gru gsum steng du bzhag/nag po'i thod pa gri gug can//
11a2(rgyud): srung dang ldan pas gsang sngags sgrub//mahA kA la ni rU pa Na/oM shrI gri bo bed dza dza ha ha/sa ma ya gegs sol//lcags kyi snying phyung zhes pas lcags la bya'o//nad kyi srung 
11a3(rgyud): bdag la ni//byang chub sems dpa'i sku gsal la//snying kha'i sa bon me 'bar bas//nad kun ma lus bsreg par bya'o//oM pi dug lum pra ti bai ro swAhA//'bru dang sman dang khu 
11a4(rgyud): ba dang /rin po che dang char dang sha/rkang dang la du dkar gsum dang //mngar gsum bcas pa'i gtor ma dang //tshu yi 'gram mam rgya mtshor dbul//me tog gdug pa bcas pa 
11a5(rgyud): dbul|na maHsar+b+ba ta thA ga ta a ba lo ki te saM b+ha ra saM b+ha ra hUM//gsang ba'i rgyud rnam par 'byed pa drag po gsum 'dul zhes bya ba las las bstan pa'i le'u gsum pa rdzogs so////
11a6(rgyud): rgya gar gyi mkhan pa de ba pu N+yA ma ti dang /lots+tsha ba dge slong chos kyi bzang pos bsgyur ba'o////rgya gar skad du/badzra pA Ni nI lAM ba ra tan+t+ra/bod skad du/lag na rdo rje gos sngon 
11a7(rgyud): po can gyi rgyud/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus cig na/gnod sbyin thams cad yongs rgya ba/shin tu dben pa/shin 
11b1(rgyud): tu yongs su dag pa na/gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can zhes bya ba/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi las kyi khro bor dbang bskur ba/mche ba gcig 
11b2(rgyud): ba|yid khros pa lta bu/mig me mdag gi phung po 'dra ba/dbugs me lce ltar 'bar ba/'jigs pa'i nga ro can/lag pa dang rkang pa klu nor rgyas kyi bus rgyan pa/klu nag po se ral khar 
11b3(rgyud): thogs pa/klu dga' dang /nyer dga' pos snyan rgyan pa/sbrul 'bar ba rin po che'i cod pan thogs pa/sbrul gdug pa 'bar ba za ba bzhin rgyas pa/smin ma dpral bar bsdus pa/ya 
11b4(rgyud): sos ma mchu mnan pa/gshin rje'i cha byad can/lce klog ltar 'gyu ba/sbrul za ba/gsus pa che pa/lag pa brdabs pa'i sgras 'jigs par byed pa klu'i rgyal po 'du ba bzang ska rags 
11b5(rgyud): bcings pa/a la la zhes sgrogs par byed pa/rgya mtsho'i zangs su dpag tshad brgyad khri bzhi stong yod pas rkang pa'i bus mor phyin pa/dbang po dang nyi ma phrag pa la rab tu khur ba/klu thad brlag 
11b6(rgyud): par byed pa'i gad rgyangs chen po dgod pas kun tu 'khrug pa byed pa/'byung po thams cad 'gugs shing 'ching bar mdzad pa/sbyor ba thams cad gcod par mdzad pa/sbyor ba thaMd bzlog 
11b7(rgyud): par mdzad pa/sbyor ba thamasacad gnon par mdzad pa/y+ton thams cad gnon par mdzad pa/y+ton thams cad ma lus pa'i bdag nyide/gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po nyi shu rtsa brgyad

12b2(rgyud): mchod gnas dngos grub myur du 'byung //gal te gzhan la phan 'dogs na//sku de sna tshogs sku ru snang //snang ba'i sgyu ma'i sku yin yang //gdug pa 'dul phyir rags par snang //sna

12b6(rgyud): sngags shes//gsum pa'i dang po gnyis pa dang //drug pa'i dang po yi ge ni//lan gnyis gong bzhin gtogs byed sngags//gnyis kyi dang po lnga dang pos//drag po dga' ba'i sa bon 
12b7(rgyud): ldan//lan gnyis ldan gyur las grub pa//gong ma bzhin du mchod byed do//le'u gsum pa'o////de nas 'phags pa lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can/sna tshogs mngon 
13a1(rgyud): @#//par grub pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/'di skad ces gsungs//oM badzra pA ni mahA yak+Sha nI lA ba ra da ha ni badzra hUM phaT/gang ting nge 'dzin rnal 'byor de//
13a2(rgyud): nyes pa kun las rab tu thar//legs pa'i yon tan thams cad thob//'bras bu yang dag mngon par 'grub//sngags ni gang yin ming yin te//gdul bya'i mtha' dang ldan pa la//
13a3(rgyud): mtha' ma las sngags mtha' dang ldan//le'u bzhi pa'o////de nas gos sngon po can 'khor lo mngon par grub pa'i ting nge 'dzinal snyoms par zhugs te 'di skad ces gsung 
13a4(rgyud): so||'byung bzhi'i sangs rgyas rab bsgom nas//so so las kyis sgom pa ni//me brgyad rlung gnyis ldan par bsam//chu brgyad dngos po gnyis yin te//de bzhin sa brgyad hUM 
13a5(rgyud): 'bras ldan//rlung brgyad me gnyis ldan pa ste//'bras bu rims kyis gnod sbyin gtso//grub pa sngags bzhin mngon par 'grub//e dang so so'i mtha' dang ldan//ma la 
13a6(rgyud): gong mas mngon par 'grub//rgyun rtsa ba'i gsang sngags bzlas//le'u lnga pa'o////de nas 'phags pa gos sngon gyis//las bcu mngon par grub pa ni//ting nge 'dzin 
13a7(rgyud): la snyoms zhugs nas//las bcu'i sbyin sreg 'di gsungs//zhi la shAn+tiM ku ru ste//rgyas pa puSh+TiM ku ru'o//dbang la ba shaM ku ru'o//drag po mA ra gsum par ldan//thab 
13b1(rgyud): khur dbyibs na lam bzhi ste//shing ni rigs dang sbyar bar bya//zhi la dkar po'i shing rnams te//rgyas la gser shing la sogs pa'o//dbang la seng ldan shing bsregs te//drag po'i 
13b2(rgyud): tsher ma can gyi shing //gzhan gyi tsan dan shing las bya//thab khung 'dab brgyad dbyibs yin te//bi d+ha sa ya/uts+tsha ta ni/mo ha ya mo ha ya/stam+b+ha ya stam+b+ha ya/ud man/ud ma na/A 
13b3(rgyud): kar+Sha ya A kar+Sha ya/kha shi ka ra kha shi ka ra/rak+sha ya rak+sha ya/mo ha b+ha ya/sar+b+ba karma ku ru hUM phaT/le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//gdul dka' ba ni 
13b4(rgyud): gdul ba'i mchog/gsang ba'i bdag po gos sngon can//drag po chen po rab 'bar ba'i//ting 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//'khor gyi klu tshogs dbang bsdus te//nyin rnams bzlog 
13b5(rgyud): pa'i cho gar gsungs//gnas khang rim pa gsum dag la//phyi rol smre bsregs dpral bar bya//bar du phyi nang khrus bas te//mnyes pa'i las kyang ci nus byas//nang du bu pa'i dbang 
13b6(rgyud): bskur bas//nyin rnams bzlog la the tshom med//kye ho nyin rnams 'dul ba'i mchog/e ma gsang chen drag po che//e ma ma rungs 'jigs pa can//e ma gsang sngags rnal

14a1(rgyud): @#//ces bka' stsal to//thams cad ldan pa'i gtor ma la//raM gyis sbyangs nas yaM gis lus//khaM gyis dkrus te dag pa la//baM las bdud rtsi chu rgyun bskyed//yaM 
14a2(rgyud): raM dbab pa'i sbyor ba bya//dza yis bkug ste gtor ma bstim//bsam gtan lha mos mchod pa dbul//de nas 'khor la 'di skad bsgos//nga ni khro bo gos sngon can//sangs rgyas 
14a3(rgyud): thams cad 'dus pa'i sku//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin gyis brlabs//dbang bskur mchog thob nga yi sku//ci ste nga yi dam 'das na//gtor ma dug gis rang bzhin gyis/
14a4(rgyud): khyod rnams thal ba rdul du rlog/gal te dam tshig ma 'das na/gtor ma ye shes bdud rtsi yi//khyed kyi sgo gsum sgrib pa sbyangs//nges par legs pa'i yon tan 'thob//oM 
14a5(rgyud): a kA ro mu khaM sarba d+harmA NAM/Ad+ya nuta pan na twata oM AHhUM swAhA//le'u brgyad pa'o////de nas dpal ldan gos sngon can//gdul dka' 'dul ba'i drag po des//
14a6(rgyud): bsam gtan 'khor la med pa la//sngags kyi nges pa bshad par bya//tad+ya thA/b+ho b+ho pan nA ga a d+hi pa ta ye/i daM nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra sha rI raM/badzra pA Ni hri da yaM/yaHsa mA 
14a7(rgyud): tI kra ma thA/uta sA da naM/b+ha bi Sh+ya ti/las thams cad pa'i sngags//las so so ba la klu 'dul sngags/tad+ya thA/bud+d+ha bud+d+hA/bi bud+d+ha bi bud+d+ha/ma hI prA p+ta/ha ra ha ra nA pa 
14b1(rgyud): na|pra ha ra bra ha ra na gA nA na/ts+tshan+da ts+tshan+da/nA ga hri da yA ni b+hin+da b+hin+da/nA ga marmA Ni bi Shi r+yan du nA ga sha ri rA Ni/bi s+pho Ta ya/nA gA na ya naM/tad+ya thA/hUM phaT/nA ga bi dA ra NI ya hUM/nA ga 
14b2(rgyud): u ta sA da nA ya hUM phaT/yaM dgas thams cad bzlog pa ni/nI lA ba se nA ya hUM phaT/a pra ti ha ta ba lA pa rA kra mA ya phaT/kr-i tAn+ta ya phaT/kri tAn+ta rU pU ya phaT/ro Sha NA ya phaT/
14b3(rgyud): trA sA nA ya phaT/kuM b+haM Dota sA da nA ya phaT/gshin rje bzlog pa'o//pre taM pi shAM tsa bi nA sha ka rA yaM phaT/yaM dgas thaMd bzlog pa'o//yak+Sha a basmA ra ba na sha ka rA ya phaT/gnod 
14b4(rgyud): sbyin la sogs pa bzlog pa'o//ha sa ha sa/d+ha ra d+ha ra/mA ra ya mA ra ya/nA sha ya nA sha ya/sarba big+h+naM bi nA sha ka rA phaT/bgegs thams cad bzlog pa'o//sarba sha trAM saM g+ha bi na sha 
14b5(rgyud): ka rA phaT/dge 'dun gyi dgra 'dul ba'o//ni lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni rA dz+nyA pa ya ti swAhA//rdo rje be con drag po che//rdo rje 'jigs pa sngags kyi bdag/rdo rje khro bo bgegs kyi gshed 
14b6(rgyud): rdo rje mi thub 'jigs pa che//rdo rje las kun grub pa che//rdo rje las rnams kun mdzad pa//sngags kyi bdag po khyod phyag 'tshal//sngags kyi ngo bo khyed phyag 'tshal//le'u dgu 
14b7(rgyud): pa'o||||de nas klu dgug pa'i sngags bstan te/rdo rje lcags kyu rlung gi sbyor bas 'grub par 'gyur//de bzhin me yis kyang ni 'dul bar nus pa yin//de ltar nus 
15a1(rgyud): @#//phyir dgug pa'i sngags ni bshad par bya//badzra pA Ni mahA yak+Sha nI lAM ba ra nA ga hrAHgrih+Na grih+Na hUM phaT/A kar+Sha ya A kar+Sha ya hUM phaT/bsam gtan drag pos 
15a2(rgyud): bzlas pa bya//bzlas pa 'di la rab 'bad bya//klu rigs du ma thams cad kun//'gugs 'gyur 'di la gzhan du min//ji srid 'gugs pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//khugs 
15a3(rgyud): par gyur pa'i klu rnams kun//zangs gzhong nang du spyan drangs te//khro bo'i che ba brjod nas ni//dam tshig bsre zhing dug rnams gzhom//sman gyi mchog gis gsos nas ni//
15a4(rgyud): gang gnod phyogs su gshegs su gsol//le'u bcu pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ste//zhi ba'i las kyis mi 'dul ba'i//klu gzir drag sngags bstan par bya//
15a5(rgyud): oM badzra pA NI mahA yak+Sha nI lAM pa ra nA ga ha na ha na hUM phaT/nA ga da ha da ha hUM phaT/nA ga s+pho Ta s+pho Ta hUM phaT/nA ga bi s+pho Ta ya bi s+pho Ta ya hUM phaT/nA ga gar+dz+dza gar+dz+dza hUM phaT/nA ga b+hin+da b+hin+da 
15a6(rgyud): hUM phaT/bsam gtan rdo rje thogs pa yis//gdul ba'i steng na gdab par bya//'og tu me yi 'khor lo bsam//me phung chen po'i ting 'dzin gyis//me 'bar klog 
15a7(rgyud): chen dkyil du gzir//yungs kar yungs nag ske tshe yis//brdeg cing khro bo'i sprul pa spro//khra spyad la sogs rtsa lnga'i gsob//gcan zan sna tshogs mche sder dang //mchu sder dang

16a2(rgyud): 'byung ba'i gtso bo'i chu yi bdag/chu brgyad mnga' bdag khyod phyag 'tshal//rlung brgyad ting 'dzin la gnasanas//mthor zhing 'bung pa'i bdag nyid che//rlung chen drag po rtsal shugs 
16a3(rgyud): ldan||rlung lha gos sngon khyod phyag 'tshal//sa yi ting 'dzin la gnas nas//mi g.yo brtan pa'i las mdzad pa//gser ldan rol mo sna tshogs ldan//se brgyad 'khor lo khyod 
16a4(rgyud): phyag 'tshal//le'u bcu gsum pa'o////de nas dpal ldan gos sngon can//phra mo'i ting 'dzin la gnas nas //'khor gyi gnod sbyin mang po kun//shin tu yid dga'
